## [2026-08-17] impl-hardening | Circuit knitting: full histogram reconstruction

cut_histogram() reconstructs the ENTIRE post-cut target-register distribution for
the LOOC-Z single-CNOT wire cut — exact (Z-diagonal), numerically verified against
the uncut simulator (Bell target = 50/50, matching uncut marginal). An attempted
multi-qubit product-Z generalization was caught as WRONG by the same verification
(a single-CNOT cut reaches one target qubit) and rejected — shipped only the
correct slice. Remaining: multi-qubit-gate/product-Z cuts need full QPD / Qiskit
Circuit Knitting Toolbox (documented). 149 py + 3 Rust green.

## [2026-08-17] impl-hardening | Canary regression/skip classification fix

The full local CI surfaced a real bug: an SDK that is installed-but-unusable (its
config dir XDG_CONFIG_HOME unwritable, e.g. root-owned ~/.config) raised an OSError
during import, which the canary misclassified as a provider REGRESSION instead of a
SKIP. Fixed at the root: TketProvider/PyQuilProvider treat OSError at SDK-init as
not-ready (fails closed as a config gap, never a regression), and scripts/ci.sh now
sets XDG_CONFIG_HOME to a writable dir (the documented deployment requirement).
Full CI green: 151 py + 3 Rust + canary 3/3 (2 skips) + correctness release-gate.

## [2026-08-17] impl-hardening | Structured logging (G11) + flaky-test fix

kubona/logging.py: JSON-lines structured ops logging (lazy stream, non-fatal emit
so logging can NEVER break the request path — the same principle as the audit
sink); HTTP gateway emits a structured request log per /rpc (server/tool/status/
duration/error); kubona serve/serve-gateway wire setup_logging. Full CI surfaced a
flaky statistical cutting test (Monte Carlo shot noise, mismatched seeds) — made it
statistically robust (consistent seed, more shots, sane tolerance). 154 py + 3 Rust
green; full local CI green twice consecutively.

## [2026-09-27] decision | Own repos are insight material, not supply

Maintainer ruling that re-scope three audit conclusions — the audited `dev-docs/` corpus exists to
**gather insights and ideas for implementation**, and several of those repos are the maintainer's own
**unfinished** work:

- **No licence task.** `chickensoup`, `milimoquantum`, `Milimo-QDD` have no `LICENSE`, but there is
  one rights-holder on both sides, so mining them needs no grant. `risks` row demoted from
  "Active — 3 LICENSE files pending" to **Closed by decision**; the surviving control is a
  release-time check *if* a derived artefact ships inside the Apache-2.0 core. Rewrote the framing on
  [[integration-topology-and-licence-discipline]], [[source-frontier-repo-audit-2026]] (ownership
  note + "unlicensed rule"), [[chickensoup]], [[mainza-consciousness]], `overview` item 7 and
  `AGENTS.md` current-state.
- **The rule that survives is about third parties** — where it always mattered: unlicensed ⇒
  all-rights-reserved ⇒ never vendored; MPL-2.0 ⇒ sidecar ([[openbao]]).
- **arXiv:2506.01536 (*Quantum Agents*) is bibliography, not an ingest gap.** It is reference 16 of
  [[source-architecture-paper]] and we have never read it. Reframed in
  [[quantum-domain-packs]] as an ambition with a citation; it enters `raw/` only if a design decision
  comes to depend on it.
- **Still open, and the only real one:** the `ghp_…` PAT in
  `dev-docs/gather_insights_from/mainza-consciousness/.git/config` — re-confirmed present on
  2026-09-27 (file is gitignored, value never written into the wiki). Revocation is the maintainer's
  action; `risks` stays **Open — revoke immediately** until confirmed, then it closes with the clone
  folder's deletion.


## [2026-09-27] verification | PAT exposure re-checked: dead token, live sink elsewhere

Re-checked the audit's headline security finding instead of repeating it (headers only; no token
value written anywhere in this repo):

- `ghp_…` in `dev-docs/gather_insights_from/mainza-consciousness/.git/config` (remote URL) →
  **HTTP 401 from `api.github.com/user`** ⇒ already revoked/expired. `git log --all -S"ghp_"` in that
  clone → empty ⇒ never committed. Only artefact left is the string in the local URL, which dies with
  the scratch folder. `risks` row: **Open → Closed by verification**.
- Root cause of the *live* exposure, found while checking: **`credential.helper=store` set globally**
  ⇒ `~/.git-credentials` holds a **live `gho_…` GitHub CLI OAuth token**, scopes
  `read:user, repo, user:email, workflow`, and **a different token** than the one `gh` keeps in the
  keyring (hash mismatch) ⇒ a stale `gh` login nobody cleans up. New `risks` row, **Open**: unset the
  `store` helper, revoke the grant in GitHub → Settings → Applications → GitHub CLI *before* deleting
  the file, rotate the Hugging Face token the same file also stores.
- Framing corrected on [[secrets-and-workload-isolation]], [[mainza-consciousness]] (⚑ block kept),
  [[source-frontier-repo-audit-2026]] finding 1 and `overview`. The lesson generalises: a secret
  written by an *ambient* tool (git helper, clone URL, IDE) is the same defect class as an agent that
  pastes a token into a config — which is why Kubona's rule is "no secret is ever written outside the
  vault", not merely "not sent to the model".


## [2026-09-27] guardrail | dev-docs/ is never pushed — verified, then enforced

Re-verified the invariant instead of trusting `.gitignore`: 0 tracked files under `dev-docs/`, 0 in
the staged set, no `.gitmodules`, no embedded-repo gitlinks (mode `160000`) tracked or staged, and
`git check-ignore -v` resolves nested clones (incl. `dev-docs/gather_insights_from/*/.git/config`) to
`.gitignore:11`. So nothing *new* can be pushed.

Two honest caveats:

1. **History.** `dev-docs/` and `.obsidian/` were tracked in the initial commit (`ab4d831`) and
   untracked the same day (`2650fe6`), so those paths exist in **pushed** history (repo is private,
   `private = true`). Content check: 2 of the 3 `dev-docs/` blobs are byte-identical to the `raw/`
   mirrors published on purpose, the third differs by 13 lines (earlier revision of the same plan),
   and a scan of all of them for tokens, emails, `/Users/mck`, `sk-`, `AKIA` found nothing. Left as a
   decision for the public flip: accept, or `git filter-repo --path dev-docs/ --path .obsidian/
   --invert-paths` + force-push.
2. **Ignore rules are advisory.** `git add -f` defeats `.gitignore`, so the rule is now enforced, not
   remembered: local `.git/hooks/pre-commit` refuses staged `dev-docs/`/`.obsidian/` paths. Proven by
   attempting a commit of a force-added `dev-docs/` file → refused, HEAD unchanged. Hook lives in
   `.git/` (never committed), so it is per-clone; CI should repeat the check.

Aside that cost three probes to settle: `git log --all -S"ghp_"` matched 10 commits, which looks
alarming and is not — they are Cline checkpoint refs (`refs/cline/checkpoints/session_…/N`) holding
my own `ghp_…` **placeholder** text from the pages above. The pushable-ref test
(`git log --branches --tags --remotes -S"ghp_[A-Za-z0-9]"`) is empty: no credential has ever been on
a branch, tag, or remote.

## [2026-09-27] cleanup | blueprint reframe + taxonomy + status sweep (81 → 82 pages)

**The reframe (maintainer decision).** The wiki's purpose was restated: it is the **blueprint**
— insight gathered so Kubona can be *shaped* — and implementation is deliberately not the
current activity. `platform/` is a **prototype scaffold** written before most insights existed.
That made a class of sentences wrong: pages describing phase gates as "implemented" were
mixing two registers, because a passing prototype test answers *is this feasible*, never
*is this the design*. Rewritten, not deleted, so the reasoning stays legible.

**What changed**
1. **New page [[questions]]** — 26 ranked open questions (P0 shape-changing / P1 subsystem /
   P2 deferrable), each with *Known*, *Missing*, and what the answer unblocks. This is the
   work-list a blueprint needs and the wiki did not have: 13 `open-question` pages previously
   had nowhere to accumulate. Q-01 (what is the prototype *for*) gates all others.
2. **Status vocabulary rebuilt.** The old 15 values (`living`, `tracked`, `proposed`,
   `experimental`, `accepted`, `blueprint-caution`, `blueprint-owner-relicensable`,
   `concept-stage`, `emerging-standard`, `append-only`, `ingested`, …) encoded *build* state and
   were unmaintainable. Replaced with 8 values describing **decidedness**:
   `settled · adopted · open-question · candidate · tracked · reference · source · living`
   (definitions in [[index]] §Status legend, rules in `AGENTS.md`). Mechanical sweep hit 81/81
   pages: `living→adopted` 25, `proposed→open-question` 11, `prior-art-reference→reference` 4,
   `integration-candidate→candidate` 4, `ingested→source` 3, plus 1 each. Result: `settled` 1 ·
   `adopted` 25 · `open-question` 13 · `candidate` 6 · `tracked` 23 · `reference` 5 ·
   `source` 4 · `living` 5.
3. **`updated:` stamped on all 81 pages**; `sources:` keys normalised to the 4 canonical
   basenames (75 pages touched — `repo-audit-2026`→`source-frontier-repo-audit-2026`,
   `architecture-paper`→`source-architecture-paper`, stray `security-pqc`/`karpathy-autoresearch`
   resolved to their real sources, and `local-dev-docs` deleted as a source key: a gitignored
   scratch path can never be a source of record).
4. **[[index]] gained a layer map** — L0 Method · L1 Control · L2 Compute · L3 Research ·
   L4 Surface · L5 Ecosystem — with each layer's decidedness, so a new idea can be *placed*
   before it is written.
5. **[[overview]]**: intro now says blueprint, not build log; "Open questions / tensions" →
   "Where the blueprint is unsettled"; "Implementation status" → **"Blueprint status &
   prototype evidence"**, split into what the prototype *has* answered and what no prototype
   test can answer (real QPU, third-party review, public repo + outside PR, business sign-off,
   the human surface).
6. **[[roadmap]]** retitled *Phases & Acceptance Gates* with a "how to read this" block: gates
   are acceptance criteria, and simulator-proven is a weaker evidence class than the gate text
   demands.
7. **[[implementation-gaps]]** retitled *Prototype Gap Register* (code facts, not design), and
   the CI row corrected — see below.

**Checks after the sweep (all zero):** dangling wikilinks · pages absent from `index.md` ·
`status` outside vocabulary · non-canonical `sources` keys · missing `updated:`. Frontmatter
intact on 82/82.

**Follow-on in the same sitting:** added `scripts/wiki-lint.py` — the checks above are now a
command instead of an anecdote. Seven check classes, negative-tested against a deliberately
broken page (all fired, exit 1; clean again after removal). `type: source` pages gained
`raw:` / `origin:` provenance instead of a self-referential `sources:` list, and the

## [2026-09-27] decision | Q-25 closed — privacy is a design posture, not repository drift

The operator settled the posture contradiction raised during the reframe. The rule on [[kubona]]
("public repo from Phase 0") and the observed state (`isPrivate: true` weeks after that gate
passed) were both recorded and flagged as a conflict; the intent behind the gap is now stated:
**the repository stays private while the blueprint is being designed, because a public repo
without a settled direction attracts contributions the project cannot direct anyone into.**

Two consequences for the blueprint, not just the repo setting:

1. **The flip condition changed shape.** It is no longer "gate passed" or a date but a property
   of the wiki itself — the blueprint must become *contributor-legible*: Q-01 answered (what
   `platform/` is for) and enough of the method layer resolved to say what to build first. That
   makes publication a **symptom of blueprint completeness**, so it moves as Q-01/Q-02/Q-04–Q-06
   move. Census at the time of the ruling: 1 `settled` against 24 `adopted`.
2. **Privacy is now load-bearing, so it needs no apology in the record.** The Apache-2.0
   commitment is unaffected; only its timing tracks the condition above.

Residual mechanical work (`dev-docs/` + `.obsidian/` sitting in pushed history) was split out as
**Q-27**, because it is an option-set — publish as-is / `filter-repo` / fresh public history —
and not part of the posture decision. Answer recorded on [[kubona]] §Posture; also updated:
[[overview]] §Repo posture, [[roadmap]] prototype-state block, `AGENTS.md`.

## [2026-09-27] verification | the release gate failed for a month because its rule existed twice

`ci.yml`'s push pipeline had **never** been green (17 recorded runs, 17 failures — re-counted
with `gh run list`; the "26" figure circulating in this session's notes counted jobs, not runs),
while `platform/scripts/ci.sh` reproduced the same pipeline locally and passed.

> ⚑ Correction: the cause recorded earlier on [[implementation-gaps]] and in [[roadmap]] — that
> `run_all()` returns `ok=False, skipped=None`, i.e. that skip classification was missing from
> `run_all()` — is **wrong**. Reproducing the hosted step verbatim locally showed `run_all()`
> already returns `skip=True` for every fail-closed provider. The real defect is duplication:
> `ci.sh` filtered skips before asserting, `ci.yml:68` asserted `all(r['ok'] …)` over all 8
> plugin-bearing providers, so the 5 legitimate skips (no credentials, no local `qvm`, optional
> SDK absent, annealing/AHS/CV program model) failed a build whose own log line read
> `canary summary: 3/3 passed`. One gate, two incompatible definitions of "pass".

Fixed by deleting the duplication instead of patching a copy: `release_gate()` in
`kubona/canary/canary.py` is now the single verdict, reached through `python -m kubona.canary
--gate`, and **both** the hosted job and `ci.sh` call it. Two properties were chosen deliberately:
an all-skip run is a **failure** (a gate that executed nothing has proven nothing — otherwise
deleting the last executable plugin would silence the gate), and non-gate modalities are probed
and reported as skips rather than filtered out of the selection, so coverage stays legible in the
log. 5 tests added, including the negative case: an injected calibration-drift regression still
exits 1, which is what proves this was a correctness fix and not a silencing. `nightly-canary.yml`
had been green the whole time — the mechanism was never in doubt, only the arithmetic around it.

**Blast radius check before claiming the fix:** `gh run view --json jobs` showed exactly one red
job (`Canary + correctness release-gate`); Rust, Python and Flutter jobs already passed. Local
`scripts/ci.sh` after the change: 159 pytest + 3 Rust passed, `release gate: OK (3 executable
providers; 5 skipped)`, `== ALL CI GATES GREEN ==`.

**Unblocked by the fix:** `scripts/wiki-lint.py --strict` is now a CI step (*Wiki structural
lint* in the `python-tests` job). It was withheld until now on the reasoning above — a step in a
pipeline that fails 17/17 adds no signal. Verified CI-safe by inspecting the check: it globs
`wiki/**` text only (no `dev-docs/`, no toolchain), and `PyYAML` arrives via the package's own
dependencies, not the `dev` extra.

## [2026-09-29] verification | the gate went green on the runner, and two claims in this session were wrong

`ci.yml` run **36508507064** (push on `main`) and **36508321976** (push on `develop`) both
completed **success** — the first green the push pipeline has ever recorded, after 17 failures.
Verified at step level rather than by the badge, because a green job whose interesting step was
skipped is the exact failure this incident is about:

* *Correctness-regression harness (release gate)* → 3 `[PASS]` (`ibm-qiskit`, `local-simulator`,
  `qiskit-aer-simulator`), 5 `[SKIP]` each with its reason, then `release gate: OK (3 executable
  providers; 5 skipped)`. The gate executed real providers on the runner; it did not pass by
  measuring nothing.
* *Wiki structural lint (blueprint invariants)* → `wiki lint · 82 pages … ✅ clean`, in CI's
  pristine checkout — confirming the check never depended on gitignored `dev-docs/`.
* Two claims corrected on [[implementation-gaps]] and [[roadmap]] while writing this entry:
  1. **The runner is not local.** CI reports **156 passed + 3 skipped**; my local figure of 159
     passed was written onto three pages as though it were the pipeline's number. Environment-free
     test counts are the kind of number that quietly becomes a promise, so the pages now name the
     environment wherever the count appears.
  2. **CI was not watching the branch where work happens.** `on.push.branches` was `[main]`, and
     this repo advances `main` by fast-forwarding develop onto it. All 17 failures were pushes on
     `main`; `develop` had *zero* runs. So the pipeline did not fail 17 times — it detected one
     class of breakage 17 times, late, because commits were never gated as they landed. A gate
     that observes only a branch someone advances by hand is a delay, not a check. `develop` and
     `workflow_dispatch` are triggers now.
* **History corroborates the root cause.** `17dc27f` ("Canary regression/skip classification fix +
  CI robustness") patched the `ci.sh` copy of the rule and left `ci.yml` alone — and failed at
  *this identical step*. That is the duplication thesis confirmed by the record rather than by my
  reading of it, and it is why the fix moved the rule into the package instead of editing either
  copy.
* **One consequence for the roadmap:** P4's acceptance criterion is "public repo + a merged
  outside PR". The Q-25 ruling (private by decision, until the blueprint is contributor-legible)
  does not contradict P4 but *defers* it — P4 cannot be met while private. Recorded on
  [[overview]] so the dependency is visible instead of latent.

> [[implementation-gaps]] — carries that date. The operator's own clock now reports
> `2026-09-29T01:46Z` and GitHub's Actions timestamps agree (`2026-09-29T01:33Z`), so the true
> date of this work is **2026-09-29**. Earlier `2026-09-27` entries elsewhere in this file are

## [2026-10-04] cleanup | Viewport Layout Regression Fix: Strict Three-Column Spatial Constraint

* **Defect Root Cause Analysis:**
  - In `platform/console/src/app/page.tsx`, `<aside aria-label="Research Copilot and Tribunal">` lacked an explicit width class (`w-96 md:w-[420px]`). In flex row layout with `shrink-0`, an unconstrained width paired with child `w-full` / long prompt pills caused the companion to compute unbounded max-content intrinsic width, starving the `flex-1 min-w-0` central observatory stage.
  - Furthermore, `scrollIntoView()` within the companion caused the window/viewport to scroll horizontally to the far right on message updates, clipping the central stage and left navigation out of the user's viewport.
* **Architecture Fixes Applied:**
  - **Explicit Responsive Aside Bounds:** Controlled the right drawer width on the `<aside>` container in `page.tsx` (`w-[380px] lg:w-[420px]` in docked mode; `w-[640px] xl:w-[700px]` in expanded salon mode), with `overflow-hidden` and `shrink-0`.
  - **Fluid Child Containment (`CopilotDrawer.tsx`):** Root container now fills its parent strictly via `w-full h-full overflow-hidden`, preventing width inflation. Added header close button (`PanelRightClose`) and lifted spatial expansion state to `page.tsx`.
  - **Safe Container Scrolling:** Replaced global `scrollIntoView()` with container-scoped `messagesContainerRef.current.scrollTo()`, eliminating all viewport-level scroll jumps.
  - **Viewport Shell Guard:** Added `w-screen overflow-hidden` to the root container in `page.tsx`.
* **Verification Clean:**
  - Next.js Turbopack production build: 100% green (`pnpm build`).
  - ESLint 9: 0 errors (`pnpm eslint --quiet`).
  - Wiki structural lint: 90 pages verified clean (`python3 scripts/wiki-lint.py --strict`).
  - Production server live on `:3000` with all three layout regions verified in HTML DOM.

## [2026-10-05] verification | Forensic Investigation & Remediation: Campaign Pre-Population & Session Accumulation

* **Forensic Audit (`new_campaign_population_investigation.md`):**
  - Traced root causes of brand-new research campaigns appearing pre-populated with finished 30-step trajectories ($R=0.7414\text{ \AA}$, $\Delta = 0.00\text{ mHa}$) and finalized manuscripts.
  - Root Causes Isolated:
    1. Automatic default synthesis on `POST /api/sessions` without configuration parameters.
    2. Missing campaign configuration modal in console UI (calling `createSession` with generic title).
    3. Title keyword fallback in `campaign_synth.go` defaulting unrecognized titles to `H2 Ground State Equilibrium`.
    4. Binary synthesis model only emitting completed 30-step trajectories with zero discrepancy.
    5. Regex chemical element extraction bug in `SpatialCanvas.tsx` (`/([A-Z][a-z]?)/g` parsing "Ground" as element `"Gr"`).
    6. Double subtitle stutter in `ObservatoryCockpit.tsx` concatenating `"Ground State Optimization"`.
    7. Absence of session deletion API causing historical test campaigns to accumulate in SQLite indefinitely.
* **Backend Remediations (`platform/core`):**
  - `session.go`: Added transactional `DeleteSession(id string) error` cascading across chat messages, campaign states, and sessions table.
  - `campaign_synth.go`: Added `SynthesizeCampaignStateWithOpts(sessionID, title, targetMolecule, fresh)` supporting fresh discovery canvases (`fresh=true` initializes empty trajectory, pending hypothesis, and clean outline).
  - `server.go`: Added `DELETE /api/sessions/{id}` endpoint and expanded `POST /api/sessions` payload with `target_molecule` and `fresh`.
  - Recompiled `kubonad` binary and restarted daemon on port 8080.
* **Frontend Remediations (`platform/console`):**
  - `NewCampaignModal.tsx`: Created Apple/Muse-style campaign creation modal with molecule presets ($H_2$, $LiH$, $BeH_2$, $H_2O$, $N_2$, $CO$, Custom), budget cap slider, and Clean Slate vs Reference Baseline toggle.
  - `app/page.tsx`: Wired `NewCampaignModal` to `⌘N`, "+ New Research Campaign", and Command Palette; added hover delete action (`Trash2`) on session items.
  - `SpatialCanvas.tsx`: Whitelisted periodic table elements and sanitized molecular targets, eliminating fictitious `"Gr"` rendering.
  - `ObservatoryCockpit.tsx`: Normalized system display names, eliminating title stutter.
  - `FlightRecorder.tsx`: Added empty-state trajectory visualization for fresh discovery campaigns awaiting first execution.
* **Verification Green:**
  - `go test ./pkg/...`: 100% pass across all packages, including new `session_test.go`.
  - `pnpm eslint --quiet`: 0 errors.
  - `pnpm build`: Next.js Turbopack optimized build 100% green.
  - Daemon and console servers live on `:8080` and `:3000`.
  - Strict wiki lint (`python3 scripts/wiki-lint.py --strict`): 90 pages exit 0.

## [2026-10-12] fix | pytest CWD-robust + Python ledger single-writer

  mirroring Go's <db>.lock (E-1a). Regression test
  test_concurrent_process_appends_keep_chain fails on old code with the
  exact fork signature, passes on new.

## [2026-10-12] hardening | dep pins fixed for CI 3.11 (pipeline caught it)
  The == pins broke CI: numpy 2.5.3 needs Python >=3.12, CI runs 3.11
  (Python-tests + release-gate jobs failed at pip install). Fixed with
  an interpreter marker (2.5.3 on >=3.12, 2.4.6 on <3.12 — the latter
  what >= floors resolved on 3.11). Pipeline green again. Lesson
  recorded: pins must satisfy the oldest supported interpreter
  (requires-python>=3.11), not just the local venv.

## [2026-10-12] fix | qir-core parse boundary + tests 4 -> 8

  - Measured: greedy 3513 + 1215 -> 3507 + 1213 (21 moves, 2 cycles, input/reverse pruned; 23s). The thin gain is the finding: greedy + local search plateaus 12% above the paper's 3136 + 480, falsifying "we just didn't try" and locating the gap in the paper's global DP over orderings/representatives (named in the SI, no recurrence given — unrecoverable, stays queued). Cancel-direction decomposition: the paper's transitions cancel ~1.3 suffix links vs our ~3 — ordering quality, same operation set, no missing structural trick identified.
  - `platform/tests/test_codesign_slice47.py`: improved pins (3507/1213/325, meta start/cycles/moves/skipped) with structural <= greedy assert, full 576-replay on a random config (trace values + op totals), asymmetry probe pin, 12-loop subset exercising multi-start/determinism/refusals (empty, bad perm, unknown/empty starts).

## [2026-10-19] decision | B5 closed: depth-9 unreachable in the paper schedule, refusal is the spec
  - SI evidence (main.tex:2298-2310): j_store is the depth from which the circuit stops decoding; j_store=5 decodes depths 2-4; the extreme j_store=9 decodes through j=8; the measured family is exactly j_store in {0,5,9} (history qubits 198/60/24, paper-measured). No SI configuration uses j_store >= 10, so no schedule invokes a depth-9+ decoder. A depth-j decoder is a Boolean function of 2(j-1) bits — depth 9's 65,536 inputs are exactly what the exhaustive proof scope excludes (divsteps.py:221). Depths-2..8 boundary stands as specified. Reopens only on new evidence (j_store >= 10 source, which would also need the unmeasured d9+ tail split).

Overwriting a stale bank unitarily is impossible in general (erasure is non-unitary; uncomputing the stale content cascades to the walk start; finite pools fail by pigeonhole). MBU-for-banks queued as the principled Q-reduction (model change). Report B1b/C6 corrected accordingly.

  - B8 RESOLVED (metrology, no code): SI main.tex:1706-1707 — the paper's offline Clifford-linear part cancels the mask at 71 positions (4,024 scanned). Multi-start found one 14%-better factoring (187 vs 219 nodes) — NOT adopted (divstep decoders, not RS path; re-pinning proven slices for ~64T; needs proper validation). Even 2x saves <200T (<0.003%). Headroom queued as micro-optimization.

Paper's 11T requires MBU (1T/AND, no uncompute) — a measured-circuit model change in the proven phase scan + feedforward, for ~125kT (1.7% of decode). Accept 42T with numbers.
  - C7 SCOPED, DEFERRED to dedicated slice: A3 still blocked; only the 'our own fixed-width packed layout' branch is actionable (labeled OURS, acceptance criteria recorded). Large build, not Phase-4 filler.

## [2026-10-20] decision | E2 (bar addendum): MBU measured circuits allowed for classical-input decode paths
  - Measurement-based uncompute (MBU: AND into temp, fan out, X-basis measure, CZ-correct on outcome) changes circuits from unitary to measured-with-feedforward. RULING: permitted for classical-input decode paths (all current slices: inputs are classical syndromes/coefficients, never superpositions), with the SAME production bar otherwise (exhaustive/basis proofs, multi-trajectory verification covering measurement randomness, temps clean, fail-closed refusal). Rationale: tower.py MBU precedent is proven (slice 11, corrections load-bearing test catches missing CZ); measurements on basis states are non-disturbing (outcomes classical); the alternative (unitary-only) costs 2x T per AND with no correctness benefit here. Explicitly OUT of scope: MBU on any future superposition-carrying path (would need a separate ruling + coherence proof). Re-litigation needs new evidence.

Deployed to EEA elim mults, BM round mults, omega mults (rs12); tower F8 + Fermat + Chien/scan untouched (CNOTs or small; out of scope).

## [2026-10-25] implementation | Phase 2: Non-Blocking Cloud QPU Queueing, Vendor Status Polling & D-Wave QUBO Dispatch

  - Non-blocking hardware dispatch & vendor status polling (`base.py`, `qiskit_provider.py`, `tket_provider.py`, `jobs.py`, `execution.py`): added `wait_for_result: bool = True` on `Provider.submit` and `poll_hardware_job(hardware_job_id)` on `QiskitProvider` (`QiskitRuntimeService.job(id).status()`) and `TketProvider` (`QuantinuumBackend.circuit_status(handle)` with tuple bitstring key normalization). When `submit_job` dispatches a hardware modality (`wait=False`), `_run` passes `wait_for_result=False` so `JobRunner` pins `hardware_job_id`, leaves the job in `running`, and releases the worker thread slot immediately instead of blocking for hours in cloud queues; `get_job_status` and `get_result` call `_refresh_deferred_hardware_job` to poll the vendor SDK and advance terminal transitions (`completed` / `failed` / `cancelled`) across restarts.
  - D-Wave QUBO dispatch (`execution.py`, `_mount.py`, `policy.py`, `QuantumDispatch.tsx`): added `submit_qubo_job` MCP tool (`execution/submit_qubo_job`, scope `execution:submit`) and wired `submit_job` to delegate JSON 2-D QUBO matrices when `provider == "dwave-annealing"` (while refusing OpenQASM circuits on annealing providers with actionable guidance).

## [2026-10-25] implementation | Phase 5: Workload Process-Group Containment, Unix Domain Socket Transport & Repository Hygiene

  - Sandbox Process-Group Containment (`Q-09`): in Python `WorkloadSandbox` (`platform/kubona/sandbox/execution.py`), launched sandboxed subprocesses with `start_new_session=True` (POSIX) and wired `subprocess.TimeoutExpired` to send `SIGKILL` to the entire process group (`os.killpg(proc.pid, signal.SIGKILL)`), preventing forked grandchild processes from surviving execution timeouts; in Go Core (`platform/core/pkg/sandbox/proc_unix.go`, `proc_windows.go`, `docker.go`), wired `setProcessGroup(cmd)` (`Setpgid: true`) and `cmd.Cancel = func() error { return killProcessGroup(cmd) }` so context cancellation/timeout kills `-cmd.Process.Pid` across container and host fallback runs. Pinned by `test_sandbox_timeout_terminates_process_group` (`test_watchdog_and_sandbox.py`) and `TestSandboxExecution` (`docker_test.go`).
  - Unix Domain Socket Transport for Local gRPC Bridge: in `platform/bridge/server.py`, added `socket_path` / `KUBONA_BRIDGE_SOCKET` support creating an authenticated `unix:///...` listening endpoint with `0600` permissions alongside TCP; in `platform/core/cmd/kubonad/main.go`, updated daemon gRPC client initialization to automatically target `unix://...` when `KUBONA_BRIDGE_SOCKET` is set or `--bridge unix://...` is passed. Tested in `test_bridge_unix_domain_socket_serving` (`test_quantum_rpc.py`).
  - Workspace Hygiene (`R-25`): added `scratch/` to root `.gitignore` under `# Local-only working files — never pushed` so local exploration documents and quickstarts remain untracked without polluting repository status.

## [2026-10-26] decision | Operator rulings: Q-01 v1 scope boundary, Q-38 unified session budget, Q-39 cached depth free lens, Q-40 L2 session mandate + escalation, Q-41 refund knobs
  - Settled five open blueprint governance questions across `wiki/entities/kubona.md`, `wiki/questions.md`, and `kubona_governance_and_operator_policy_rulings.md`:
    1. Q-01 (v1 Scope Boundary): Formally bound v1 to 5 executable backends (`local-simulator`, `qiskit-aer-simulator`, `ibm-qiskit`, `quantinuum`, `dwave-annealing`) and 4 core modalities (`molecular_chemistry`, `combinatorial_optimization`, `circuit_codesign_and_decoding`, `spatial_generative_synthesis`); Rigetti, IonQ, AWS Braket, Pulser, Strawberry Fields, periodic crystal materials, and dynamic weight retraining explicitly deferred to v2. `wiki/entities/kubona.md` promoted from `open-question` to `adopted`.
    2. Q-38 (World Labs Atlas Spend Accounting): Adopted unified single-book session budget ceiling ($150 default) with sub-category spend tagging (`media_atlas` vs `quantum_qpu`); rejected separate media quota subsystem to prevent budget laundering and preserve single-book CAS financial governance.
    3. Q-39 (Cached-Depth Lens Billing): Adopted "Looking is free for cached views" rule: switching to an existing depth asset or session manifest returns `$0.00` (`cached: true`); fresh generative synthesis and 3D chisel mutations bill `$0.50`.
    4. Q-40 (L2 Autonomy Mandates): Settled session-level AP2 mandate authorization for iterative subcircuits under the session ceiling, coupled with high-watermark single-job escalation (jobs exceeding `$10.00` or requesting foreign export jurisdictions require an explicit per-job cryptographic mandate card).
    5. Q-41 (Refund Policy Knobs): Codified two-tier refund authority (automatic 100% spend release on system/bridge failures; discretionary post-settlement refunds restricted to Tenant Admin Ed25519 signatures within a 72-hour window, with partial refunds supported bounded by reserved spend).

## [2026-10-27] decision | Phase 0 complete + Phase 1 security slice landed (uncommitted)

  - Phase 0: fidelity contract ships in the kubonad image (build context platform/, Dockerfile-specific ignore, KUBONA_CONTRACTS_DIR) and the image boots health/ready 200; MCP catalog regenerated to 37 tools and reviewed; plan_dual_model_packing exported; console lint 0; slice-coverage guard parses every workflow; thesis-scale slice60 moved to nightly; bridge RPC tests + local ci.sh parity; false README/AGENTS claims corrected.

  - Verification: Go all green (build/vet/test), Python fast 785 passed/3 skipped, bridge 58/1, console 272 + lint 0 + tsc 0, Rust 23, wiki-lint clean, Docker boot + bridge auth live. No commit/push.

## [2026-10-27] decision | Phase 1b: signed session assertion, signed imports, loop tenancy, atomic idempotency, container sandbox CI

  - Bridge: the session mirror's ceiling/spent/tenant now carries an HMAC-SHA256 assertion from Go (KUBONA_BRIDGE_TOKEN), verified by a server interceptor with a shared Go/Python vector; self-declared money/tenant is refused.
  - Import: manifests are operator-signed over the section digests; production refuses unsigned manifests; tampered-digest and unsigned-production pins added.
  - Loops: listings filter by caller tenant; actions resolve the loop and bind its session; unknown ids stay 404.
  - Money: idempotency is claimed atomically (find_or_create under the store lock) before spend; refunds emit session_spend_refunded when a ledger is wired; concurrent same-key test.
  - Sandbox: a sandbox-container CI job builds the runner image and executes the live container path (verified locally).
  - Verification: Go all green + vet clean; Python fast 786 passed/4 skipped; bridge 60/1; Rust 23; console 272 + lint 0 + tsc 0; wiki-lint clean.

## [2026-10-27] decision | Phase 3a: live H2 anchor, fail-closed comparator, claim theater removed

  - H2 anchor is a live PySCF RHF+FCI solve at the requested bond length (-1.137270 Ha at 0.7414 A; -0.948641 at 2.0 A); tests pin the value, geometry dependence, and provenance. The 65 mHa table is no longer served.
  - Comparator toolchain absence returns ok=False / verification_unavailable with no synthesized axioms; the AST path is not a pass. Tribunal no longer counts static AST as Lean verification.
  - Theater: 3D HUD shows UNVERIFIED and rho = — without computed data; Lean widget requires a real tactic/axiom/sorryAx receipt; manuscript deposit re-verifies sign-off/content hash; peer review uses abs(discrepancy).
  - Remaining Phase 3b: HF fallback label, calibration timestamps, autoresearch nonce/constants, absent model artifact.

## [2026-10-27] decision | Phase 4a: console build gate + security headers

  - A production `next build` with NEXT_PUBLIC_KUBONA_CONSOLE_TOKEN set now fails closed (verified with a leaked token); KUBONA_ALLOW_DEV_TOKEN=1 is the explicit local-demo escape hatch.
  - next.config.ts emits CSP (frame-ancestors none, object-src none, base-uri/form-action self, scheme-wide connect for runtime API/SSE), X-Frame-Options DENY, nosniff, strict referrer, and a restrictive Permissions-Policy.
  - Go CORS adds Vary: Origin so shared caches cannot serve one origin's ACAO to another.

## [2026-10-27] verification | Dependency and toolchain remediation

  - Next 16.3.8 -> 16.4.3 and eslint-config-next 16.4.3 (critical advisories); undici pinned to 8.12.3 via the pnpm-workspace override (dev-only jsdom path); go-ipld-prime 0.21 -> 0.24.

## [2026-10-27] decision | Phase 3b: real calibration age, spec-only model manifest, labelled template constants

  - Fidelity calibration age is parsed from calibrated_at (missing/unparseable = stale). The shipped 2026-10-01 QPU profiles are now correctly stale and refuse admission; tests refresh fixture epochs and pin the stale-refusal separately.

  - Autoresearch template entanglement constants carry measurement_source=template_constant and measured=false.

## [2026-10-27] decision | Phase 4b: quantum quote freshness binding

  - QuantumDispatch binds a quote to {session, provider, QASM/QUBO, shots}; editing any field makes it stale and disables Dispatch until re-estimation. Missing estimated_usd renders UNPRICED and cannot dispatch (was treated as $0, skipping the mandate gate).
  - Dependabot: all 9 open alerts cleared by the dependency/toolchain slice (GitHub API returns 0 open).

## [2026-10-27] verification | Full CI gate green + branch protection enabled

## [2026-10-27] decision | Phase 3c: HF fallback provenance + optional Atlas/A2A gates

  - HF summaries carry source=live|fallback and estimated=true on the offline sovereign catalog; GetModelDetails tries live first and only falls back on a network error (404 still refuses). Tests pin the provenance.

Production defaults to multi-lab auth reads unless KUBONA_SINGLE_LAB=1; compose declares single-lab and KUBONA_ENV=production. Feature-gate tests added.

## [2026-10-27] verification | Latest green gate after governance + HF provenance

  - CI run 40458725971 on 629dbc0: all 15 checks green, including the production compose stack with KUBONA_ENV=production, the sandbox container job, and the HF provenance changes.
  - Open decisions narrowed to Q-50 (money authority/exact unit), Q-51 (refund policy), Q-56 (console auth/key custody); recommended defaults are on the audit page.

## [2026-10-27] decision | Q-50/Q-51/Q-56 approved; single-authority money + canonical mandates

  - Q-50: Go SQLite is the authority for API/human/quantum dispatch. Go-verified QuantumSubmit now passes spend_prepaid to Python, so the mirror is not charged twice and does not refund; Go reconciles the reservation from the status poll.
  - Q-51: Go releases a failed/cancelled reservation only when no hardware_job_id is pinned; post-vendor failures keep the charge. Python skips its own refund for Go-prepaid jobs.
  - Q-56: the mandate card signs canonicalJobBinding(session, program) via authorizeSpend (server-verified) and sends the full mandate JSON; the Go proxy verifies tenant/program/budget/expiry when a signed mandate is present. Baked-bearer builds already fail.
  - Residuals: integer micro-USD migration, passkey/non-extractable custody, Q-40 mandatory-mandate policy.

## [2026-10-27] decision | Ledger audit retention + production VerifyChain surface

  - DeleteSession no longer deletes ledger_events: the hash chain and the signed session_deleted receipt outlive the content rows. The receipt is signed by the operator signer and keeps the original tenant attribution (AppendEventWithTenant).
  - GET /api/ledger/verify (bearer) exposes VerifyChain for a session or the whole ledger; a tamper reports ok=false with faults. This is the production caller the audit found missing.
  - Tests: cascade now asserts the surviving chain verifies clean; a tamper test reports a fault; full Go -race suite green.

## [2026-10-27] verification | Ledger retention slice green

  - CI run 40467016495 on 39cc5c9: all 15 checks green. Audit retention (signed session_deleted receipt, surviving chain) and GET /api/ledger/verify are landed.

## [2026-10-27] decision | LLM budget gate: full scan, fail-closed, all cloud roles

  - llmSpendSummary scans the full llm_spend_estimated ledger (no 10k LIMIT) and returns query/scan/JSON errors; checkLLMBudget refuses when the total cannot be verified instead of treating it as zero.
  - The gate now trips when the active provider OR any role assignment is non-local (a local-active config with a cloud role used to bypass the cap).
  - cost summary returns a typed 500 when spend cannot be verified; added the (event_type,id) index for the scan.

## [2026-10-27] decision | Ledger v2: metadata-committed, canonical, versioned

  - New events use hash_version=2: tenant/label/timestamp/type + a canonical-payload hash are inside the digest, so relabelling or moving an event breaks the chain.
  - v1 rows keep verifying with the legacy digest (versioned verifier). Import/export carry hash_version + signature_domain.
  - External mandate events use signature_domain=mandate-v1 and verify the reconstructed SpendMandate canonical message (fixes the v1 domain mismatch); opaque x402 proofs are recorded, never claimed verified.
  - Tests: metadata tamper, v1 compatibility, external mandate signature, opaque proof; full Go -race suite green.

## [2026-10-27] decision | Tenant-scoped event attribution

  - resolveTenantLocked now falls back to a tenant row when the session id is a tenant id, so tenant-scoped events keep tenant/lab attribution instead of defaulting to "default" (audit GOD-07). Test pins the row.

## [2026-10-27] decision | Integer micro-USD books for sessions and tenants

  - Added pkg/money (exact micro-USD, rounding/overflow/invalid refusal) and micro columns for sessions/tenants with an idempotent legacy backfill. Micro is authoritative; REAL remains a compatibility projection updated in the same statement.
  - Session/tenant reserve/release/refund now use guarded integer arithmetic (no epsilon), and reads project micro to USD at the boundary. A 10k x $0.000001 test proves exactness and zero dust.
  - Remaining: job/loop reservation columns are still REAL (converted at the session/tenant boundary); migrate those next.

## [2026-10-27] decision | Job reservations join the micro-USD books

  - jobs gain reserved_amount_micro_usd with an idempotent legacy backfill; SubmitJob and RecordQuantumBridgeJob write micro, releaseReservation reads/zeros micro and releases the exact converted amount, and import restores micro from the manifest.
  - Remaining REAL money: loop reserved/consumed amounts (next), and the REAL projection columns (remove after all reads migrate).

## [2026-10-27] decision | Loop spend joins the micro-USD books

  - loops gain reserved/consumed micro columns with an idempotent backfill; SaveLoopState, AddLoopConsumed, and ClaimLoopSpend use integer arithmetic; Get/List project micro to USD. Import restores micro.
  - A 10k x $0.000001 drawdown test yields an exact $0.99 refund.
  - Remaining: remove the REAL compatibility projection after all reads migrate, and passkey/Q-40/autoresearch residuals.

## [2026-10-27] decision | All spend reads use micro; REAL is an atomic compatibility projection

  - Job get/list/export and outstanding-reservation blockers read reserved_amount_micro_usd; GetLoopState reads loop micro columns. No production read path uses the REAL money columns anymore.
  - REAL columns remain only as a compatibility projection updated in the same statement as micro; they cannot drift at write time. Dropping them is a separate schema-only cleanup (tests/ops still read them).

## [2026-10-27] decision | Q-40: signed mandate required before human hardware dispatch

  - /api/quantum/jobs now requires a full signed mandate for non-simulator, non-dry-run submissions BEFORE calling the bridge; a missing/bare/forged mandate refuses with no enqueue.
  - The pre-call check binds signature/tenant/exact program/expiry; the post-estimate check binds budget and cancels the bridge job if the estimate exceeds it.
  - Simulators and dry-run estimates stay mandate-free. The console already sends the canonical mandate.

## [2026-10-27] decision | Autoresearch refuses forgeable self-reported scores in production

  - All current harnesses are marked SelfReported (the artifact runs in the same process as the evaluator; it can forge/patch the score).
  - InitializeBaseline/Step refuse a self-reported harness when KUBONA_ENV=production unless KUBONA_AUTORESEARCH_ALLOW_SELF_REPORTED=1 is set for research-only runs. A measured harness (separate evaluator process) does not implement the marker and is unaffected.
  - The nonce fence remains an output delimiter, not a security boundary.

## [2026-10-27] decision | HTTP panic boundary, request IDs, DB-aware readiness

  - Handler wraps every route with requestIDAndRecovery: a panic is logged with the stack and a request id, and returns a typed 500 instead of killing the daemon.
  - A2A task goroutines recover panics, log, and mark the task failed.
  - /api/ready now pings SQLite (2s) in addition to the disk guard; a closed DB is 503, not "ready".
  - Remaining observability: metrics/tracing and structured JSON logs.

## [2026-10-27] decision | Prometheus metrics surface

  - GET /metrics (bearer) exposes kubona_http_requests_total, duration sum/count by method+status (low cardinality, no path/tenant labels), and inflight requests.
  - Instrumentation wraps recovery so a panicking request is counted as 500, never 200; a test pins the ordering.
  - Remaining observability: tracing and structured JSON logs.

## [2026-10-27] verification | Metrics slice green; auth-session test flake observed

  - CI run 40557624085 initially failed only TestAuthSessionsListAndRevoke (cookie auth returned 401 after revoke); the same commit passed on rerun, and the test passed 20x locally with -race. Recorded as a CI flake to monitor, not a code change: the gate is green on the rerun.

## [2026-10-27] decision | Structured JSON logging on the request/panic paths

  - logging.Setup sets slog: JSON by default in production, text in dev, with KUBONA_LOG_FORMAT/KUBONA_LOG_LEVEL overrides.
  - Every HTTP request logs method/path/status/duration_ms/request_id; panics log method/path/request_id/panic/stack; A2A panics log task_id/stack.
  - A test captures the JSON handler and level override. Legacy log.Printf call sites remain for a later sweep; tracing remains.

## [2026-10-27] decision | Q-56: lab-admin session authorizes admin writes (no client key after reload)

  - AdminAction gains ViaSession (json:"-", server-set only). verifyAdmin skips the client signature only for that marker after tenant/status checks; the API sets it only when the httpOnly session's fingerprint equals the tenant's current admin key.
  - bindAdminActionOrSession accepts a signed envelope (machine/bearer) OR the lab-admin session (console after reload) and binds the exact params hash. Applied to ceiling, status, autonomy, tenant/session refund, grant revoke. Admin-key rotation and mandate signing remain signature-only.
  - Console APIs accept an optional action; LabOpsPanel signs when the browser key matches, otherwise omits the envelope and relies on the session. Test pins the no-envelope fallback; a Go test pins cross-lab refusal.

## [2026-10-27] decision | Measured ansatz-search evaluator (artifact cannot forge the score)

  - Stage 1 is the only stage that imports the artifact; it returns a QASM circuit and nothing else.
  - Stage 2 runs in a separate sandbox invocation with no artifact mounted; it parses the QASM and computes energy/Meyer-Wallach/depth/2q itself.
  - The ansatz harness no longer implements SelfReported, so production runs it without the research opt-in. The other four harnesses remain self-reported and production-blocked.
  - Tests: real two-stage evaluation on the default VQE ansatz; self-reported gate test now pins QAOA as blocked and ansatz as measured.

## [2026-10-27] decision | Measured QAOA evaluator

  - Stage 1 imports qaoa_schedule.py only to extract finite gamma/beta lists; stage 2 runs in a separate sandbox with no artifact and builds/simulates the 8-node MaxCut circuit, computing the approximation metric itself.
  - QAOA no longer implements SelfReported, so production runs ansatz + QAOA without the opt-in.
  - Still self-reported/blocked: error-mitigation (analytic formula), compilation-pass (no equivalence check yet), correctness-regression (computes a fixed Bell state and ignores the artifact). These need real measured implementations before production use.

## [2026-10-27] decision | All five autoresearch harnesses measured

  - correctness-regression now parses the submitted QASM as data and simulates it; the trusted stage scores fidelity against |Phi+>. An empty artifact is invalid, not a perfect Bell state.
  - compilation-pass runs the candidate pass manager in stage 1 only; stage 2 checks unitary equivalence to QFT-4 before scoring depth x 2Q gates. A cheaper non-equivalent circuit is refused.
  - error-mitigation validates scale factors only in stage 1; stage 2 runs a real Aer density-matrix depolarizing simulation at each scale and Richardson-extrapolates the GHZ-4 ZZ observable.
  - No shipped harness implements SelfReported. The production gate remains for future harnesses and is tested with a fake.

## [2026-10-27] decision | Real money projection dropped

  - tenants/sessions/jobs/loops no longer create or write spend_ceiling_usd, spent_usd, spend_limit_usd, current_spend_usd, reserved_amount, or consumed_amount.
  - OpenLedger backfills micro from the legacy REAL columns once, then drops them (SQLite >=3.35); USD JSON fields are projected from micro on read.
  - Migration test proves backfill values and column removal; all package tests use micro-only fixtures.

## [2026-10-27] decision | Legacy log.Printf sites removed

  - All 53 non-test log.Printf/Println sites in kubonad, autoresearch, tribunal, campaign synth, server, loop settle, and edge auth now emit structured slog fields.
  - Startup refusal log.Fatalf sites remain fatal exits, not ambient logging.
  - The tribunal telemetry test now captures the process slog handler and still asserts every turn field.

## [2026-10-27] decision | W3C trace context on every request

  - New pkg/tracing continues a valid inbound traceparent or mints a 128-bit trace and 64-bit span; all-zero/malformed input is rejected and replaced.
  - The HTTP wrapper returns traceparent + X-Trace-Id, puts the span in request context, and logs method/path/status/duration with trace/span/parent ids (debug 2xx, warn 4xx, error 5xx).
  - Panic logs carry the trace id. No collector is required; spans are structured slog records.

## [2026-10-27] decision | Calibration and rate-card refresh tools

  - kubona.fidelity.refresh validates a live provider snapshot (positive T1/T2, T2 <= 2*T1, non-negative errors), stamps calibrated_at/version/source, and atomically updates fidelity-devices.json. Unmeasurable input refuses; dry-run is default.
  - kubona.cost.rate_card validates positive rates, USD, ISO verified date, vendor URL, and plan notes, then atomically replaces a provider schema's pricing object. Dry-run is default.
  - Both are operator-gated: live calibration needs the vendor key, and vendor pricing is not scraped. Tests run without keys.

## [2026-10-27] decision | Session-import key rotation

  - Manifests now name a key_id. KUBONA_IMPORT_HMAC_KEYS holds comma-separated kid=key pairs; KUBONA_IMPORT_HMAC_KEY_ID selects the signing key; the legacy single key and API token remain verifier fallbacks.
  - Verification uses the named key and refuses an unknown/rotated-out kid; keyless legacy manifests try every configured key.
  - Tests cover active-key export, both keys verifying, unknown kid refusal, and legacy keyless import.

## [2026-10-27] decision | P2 integrity and fail-closed sweep

  - Mandates: API refuses empty job_hash, non-finite/non-positive budgets, and TTL > 24h; Verify rejects an unbound or NaN-budget mandate only after signature verification (no mismatch oracle).
  - Loop spend release CASes reserved+consumed (retry on movement), so a concurrent consumption cannot cause a stale over-refund.
  - Parser: unknown registers refuse instead of aliasing raw indices; reset refuses in unitary-only contexts.
  - Autoresearch: watchdog ABORT/PAUSE stops before refinement; shadow comparison handles negative scores; halt returns best-first history.
  - Canary snapshot_hash now seals calibration; fidelity gate refuses unknown devices and over-capacity qubit counts; secret get(required=True) raises.
  - Library: validation proofs must be non-phantom and resolve against a wired ledger; saves are atomic+fsynced. Critic requires at least one applicable mutant.
  - Qiskit Runtime uses ibm_quantum_platform (legacy channel via env override); cancel_job attempts vendor recall and the latent ProviderError NameError is fixed.

## [2026-10-27] decision | Bridge trace propagation

  - The gRPC client chain injects the inbound W3C traceparent into outgoing metadata, so a human dispatch and its bridge work share one trace id. Background jobs with no request span are unchanged.

## [2026-10-27] decision | More fail-closed boundaries

  - Atlas operation ids are bounded to [A-Za-z0-9_-]{1,128} before URL interpolation or filesystem use; invalid ids refuse at the handler, fetch helper, and webhook resolver.
  - Chat writes accept only user/assistant roles; a client-supplied system role is refused before persistence (internal system notices still use the manager directly).
  - Grant revocation registry reads and corrupt rows fail closed (API 503, admission refuses); grant budget NaN/Inf is refused.
  - Cleaned wiki/questions.md duplicate sections and marked the audit decision table resolved.

## [2026-10-27] decision | Global request-body ceiling

  - Every HTTP request is capped at 64 MiB (KUBONA_MAX_REQUEST_BODY_BYTES overrides within 1 MiB..512 MiB); oversized Content-Length gets a typed 413 `request_too_large` before any handler work, and chunked bodies are still bounded by MaxBytesReader.
  - Added the code to the CSV + Go registry and a test that oversize chat writes never persist.

## [2026-10-27] decision | Disk guard covers every spend rail

  - Lens dispatch, quantum submit, workflow run, memory retain, and session import now refuse with storage_blocked before decoding or spending when the disk guard is blocked. The disk-gate test pins all five.

## [2026-10-27] decision | Mandate verification replay is idempotent

  - Re-verifying the exact signed mandate returns the original receipt and obligation (replayed:true) instead of appending another ledger event; a missing obligation is healed. Public verification with a fresh valid mandate still appends exactly one receipt.

## [2026-10-27] decision | Supervisor abort seals its own receipt

  - /api/supervisor/abort now appends a signed `supervisor_abort` event carrying the session, aborted count, and reason, and returns that receipt. It no longer returns whichever event happened to be last.

## [2026-10-27] decision | Memory eviction is tenant-scoped

  - Hindsight capacity/eviction now filters on bank + tenant + readership, so one lab filling its bank cannot evict another lab's memories. Persistence errors on retain still need a typed path.

## [2026-10-27] decision | Message rewrite CAS is atomic

  - RewriteUserMessage's expected-content compare now lives in the UPDATE ... AND content = ?; a concurrent edit loses (ErrContentMismatch) instead of silently last-writer-winning between a pre-check and write.

## [2026-10-27] decision | Honest tribunal verdict + metasearch copies

  - The console tribunal recorder no longer labels a quorum as COMMITTED_TO_LEDGER; it returns QUORUM_MET with ledger_committed:false because it writes no ledger event.
  - Metasearch List/Get return deep copies and approve/reject require a named human signer.

## [2026-10-27] decision | Suspension stops execution

  - Suspending a tenant now cancels every unsettled job in its sessions and pauses every active autoresearch engine owned by that lab. Reactivation remains admin-signed; dispatch was already refused while suspended.

## [2026-10-27] decision | JWKS cache never holds the lock over I/O

  - fetchAtlasJWKS reads under RLock, fetches without a lock, and stores under Lock. A hung JWKS endpoint no longer serializes every webhook request; concurrent refreshes may duplicate one fetch.

## [2026-10-27] decision | Output downloads are SSRF-checked

  - downloadFile validates scheme/host, requires https + non-private resolution in production, revalidates every redirect, caps the body at 64 MiB, and validates Atlas asset ids before URL interpolation. Dev loopback fixtures remain allowed.

## [2026-10-27] decision | Import verifies event hashes and canonicalizes v1 payloads

  - Import recomputes every event's v1/v2 entry hash before insert; v1 payloads re-encoded by JSON round-trips are canonicalized when that reproduces the recorded hash, otherwise the import refuses.
  - Chain-domain signatures are verified over the recomputed digest during import (external mandate domains remain read-time verified). Tests pin tamper refusal and a pretty-printed v1 manifest importing into a clean VerifyChain.

## [2026-10-27] decision | Console science inputs are bounded

  - triage electrons/orbitals/charges, krylov matrix dimension + time step, shadow qubits/snapshots/observables, QEC logical qubits/gate counts/error rate, and memory max_hops now return typed 400s outside hard bounds. The 40-qubit/10^9-snapshot class of DoS is refused before allocation.

## [2026-10-27] decision | Go QIR fallback labels its real profile

  - The pure-Go fallback emits `qir_profiles="custom"` / inttoptr IR, so it now reports `CustomProfile-Estimate` instead of claiming BaseProfile-Estimate. The Rust core remains the BaseProfile path.

## [2026-10-28] verification | Live IBM calibration refresh

  - Operator key was supplied from the gitignored dev-docs store into `.env` (mode 600; never printed/committed).
  - Qiskit Runtime 0.50 / qiskit 2.5: channel `ibm_quantum_platform`; the retired `ibm_brisbane` contract backend was replaced with `ibm_fez`, and the provider now falls back to the first operational hardware backend if a name retires.
  - Live medians (156q Heron r2, backend update 2026-10-28T04:28:02Z): T1 144.19 µs, T2 99.35 µs, 1q error 0.000293, 2q error 0.002517, readout 0.01001. fidelity-devices.json refreshed via the validated `--write` path; no QPU job was submitted.
  - Fixed Qiskit 2.5 qubit-property parsing (T1/T2/readout are per-qubit accessors; some qubits lack T2) and excluded virtual/measurement gates from the 1q error median.
  - MCP cost and execution now share one process-wide SessionStore; a session created by one was previously invisible to the other's import-time cache.

## [2026-10-28] decision | Local model discovery is parallel and honest

  - Registry probes the configured ports concurrently (bounded pool) instead of 11 sequential 2 s timeouts, and both adapters require HTTP 200 for `available` (a 401/404 response is not a working server).

## [2026-10-28] decision | Python ledger reads are tenant-bound and lock-consistent

  - Pre-label rows are no longer ambient-readable: a named requester must match the file's tenant; ambient (None) sees public rows only.
  - Reads take a shared flock (no torn tail), and appends fsync before close.

## [2026-10-28] decision | Session store appends are fsynced and record_job is atomic

  - record_job now does its read-modify-write under the exclusive lock (concurrent job ids no longer overwrite each other), and every JSONL append flushes+fsyncs before returning.

## [2026-10-28] decision | JSON stores are atomic and locked

  - Added kubona.jsonstore (per-path thread+flock exclusive, temp+fsync+replace). LabRegistry and IdentityRegistry now reload under the lock before mutate and write atomically; concurrent lab creates are tested.

## [2026-10-28] verification | IBM Quantum documentation applied to the provider

  - Reviewed the IBM guide index and the execution-critical pages: quickstart/install, account initialization and instance auto-selection, Sampler quickstart + options, transpilation/ISA, job limits, maximum execution time, usage/cancellation, cost management, calibration/benchmarking, local testing, and the retired-QPU table.
  - Applied: local `generate_preset_pass_manager` ISA transpilation before Sampler; client-side `executor_sampler.Sampler` for qiskit-ibm-runtime 0.50; `max_execution_time` (default 600 s, env-bounded 1..10800); <=10M executions / <=5M 2q / backend-capacity preflight; `least_busy` fallback; optional instance CRN; shots cap 1M; cancel no longer treats IBM's None return as rejection.
  - Read-only live check: Bell circuit transpiled against ibm_fez to `{'rz','sx','cz','measure','barrier'}` (ISA; no job submitted).
  - Note: Open Plan is 10 QPU minutes per 28-day window; no QPU job/spend was authorized or submitted.

## [2026-10-28] fix | IBM provider test/version fallback and instance binding

  - qiskit-ibm-runtime <0.50 (and the CI stub) has no executor_sampler; fall back to legacy SamplerV2 while keeping ISA transpilation when the backend exposes a target.
  - Kept the historical "positive int" shots error while adding the 1M upper bound (W2 regex compatibility).
  - Retrieved the Open Plan instance CRN via service.instances() and stored it as KUBONA_IBM_QISKIT_INSTANCE in .env (mode 600, never printed/committed); the service now binds the instance explicitly instead of relying on auto-selection.

## [2026-10-28] verification | IBM docs link hub for agents

  - Added [[ibm-quantum-platform]]: canonical live IBM guide URLs (compute service, instances, Sampler/options, transpile/ISA, job limits, max execution, usage/cost, monitoring, calibration, retired QPUs, local testing, errors) plus the 2026-10-28 Kubona provider snapshot and agent rules.
  - Updated [[qiskit]] to point at the hub and to describe the client-side Sampler (0.50) rather than only SamplerV2.

## [2026-10-28] decision | No unhardened warm sandbox container

  - Removed EnsureWarmRunner: construction no longer starts a persistent privileged/networked `tail -f /dev/null` container that nothing executed in. Every sandbox run is the existing ephemeral `docker run --rm --read-only --cap-drop=ALL --network=none` invocation (runsc optional via KUBONA_SANDBOX_RUNTIME).

## [2026-10-28] decision | Workflow ledger failure is terminal and visible

  - A workflow_step receipt failure now marks the workflow failed and returns ok=False; the workflow can no longer remain "running" after a successful step whose audit receipt could not be written.

## [2026-10-28] verification | First live IBM QPU run under the 2-minute cap

  - Submitted a 100-shot transpiled Bell circuit to ibm_marrakesh; job dbgva40te5oc738j0ui0 completed with QPU usage 2 seconds. Counts: 11:52, 10:44, 00:3, 01:1.
  - Honest verdict: the hardware path (ISA → Sampler → job → result) works, but the distribution is not a clean Bell; q1 readout error was ~15.7% yet 44% |10> needs layout/calibration investigation. No fidelity/advantage claim.
  - An earlier ibm_fez attempt was queued >15 min and canceled with 0 usage. KUBONA_IBM_MAX_EXECUTION_SECONDS=120 is now set in the operator .env, so future runs cannot exceed 2 QPU minutes.

## [2026-10-28] verification | Platform-governed IBM dispatch proven

  - Go /api/quantum/jobs → bridge → ibm-qiskit completed under a tenant-admin mandate: job 6631e996179d48bb, IBM dbh02i9t9uhs73c93fk0, ibm_marrakesh, 100 shots, 2 s usage, counts 00:52/11:40/10:6/01:2.
  - Prereqs: air-gap off via /api/llm/settings, bridge env for IBM token/instance/backend/max-execution, canonical job hash + integer-valued budget in the admin params hash.
  - Finding recorded: estimate $8.000568 was reserved; completion keeps the estimate instead of settling to measured usage (~$3.20 at the card rate). Provider now returns usage_seconds; Go settlement is the follow-up.
  - Env: KUBONA_IBM_QISKIT_BACKEND override and compose passthrough for IBM credentials; QPU cap stays 120 s.

## [2026-10-28] decision | QPU reservations settle to measured usage

  - Provider completion now returns usage_seconds + actual_usd (per-second card, 1 s minimum).
  - Go SyncQuantumBridgeJobStatus parses result.actual_usd and CAS-settles the job reservation to the measured amount, releasing reserved-actual to tenant and session; zero usage releases the full reservation. No double-release (CAS on the reserved value).
  - Go test covers 8.00 reserved -> 3.20 actual across job, session, and tenant books.

## [2026-10-28] investigation | Marrakesh direct-run anomaly

  - Direct `Circuit` and governed QASM produce identical ISA circuits (initial layout q0/q1; rz/sx/cz/measure; ideal Aer 00/11), so conversion is not the cause.
  - Governed rerun on ibm_marrakesh produced 92% 00/11; the 44% |10> direct result did not reproduce. Job records do not retain the physical layout, so bad-pair/readout remains suspected but unproven. Future hardware jobs should log the selected layout.


## [2026-10-28] decision | Hardware jobs retain their transpiler layout

  - QiskitProvider returns the logical→physical initial/final layout on submission/results; the deferred job meta stores it, and the bridge status payload exposes it. Go keeps it in result_json. Future Marrakesh-style anomalies can be tied to the actual physical pair instead of guessed.

## [2026-10-28] decision | Tenant list is auth-scoped

  - GET /api/tenants is anonymous only when the store actually has one tenant; multi-lab requires a session/bearer, and a lab session sees only its own row. This closes the public exposure of admin key ids, ceilings, and spend totals.
